  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is non-negotiable for T5: server.py wrap + task_processor wrap + task_claim.py downgrade + queue generators/output.py downgrade + standalone RunSummary flush must land in ONE commit. Splitting them leaves the tree in a state where either (a) the claim line still leaks raw UUIDs alongside the new friendly anchor, or (b) standalone HeadlessTaskQueue.main() goes silent, or (c) production emits two anchor pairs per task. The contextvars.ContextVar nesting is the hinge \u2014 if it isn't a real ContextVar, every production task doubles. T4's outer-anchor regression test is the gate; run it before landing T5. Phase 3 tasks (T6-T11) are independent and can ship in any order after T5. T12 runs last and includes a throwaway repro script that MUST be deleted after it passes. Do NOT create new test files beyond the three in T4. Preserve all ffmpeg_fallback anomaly essentials, all debug_block callers (~50), and all model-switch billing telemetry at model_ops.py:125/:173/:194 \u2014 only wrap the model switch in a DebugCard for debug-mode expansion, don't touch the essentials. The log interceptor at core.py:287-326 demands that all lifecycle output flow through the module-level essential/success/error/debug functions \u2014 never print, never sys.stdout. When wrapping server.py:471-589 preserve the retry/requeue branches and the existing _display.on_task_done() finally at :586-589 \u2014 do not restructure the outer try/finally. Friendly id format is {short_task_type_slug}#{uuid_last8}; raw UUIDs still go to the DB via task_id= kwargs on logger calls.",

Never let diagnostics break the worker loop.\n+                            pass\n+            except OSError:\n+                # If any exception while checking, keep prior truthiness\n+                pass\n+\n+            with queue.queue_lock:\n+                task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                if is_success:\n+                    task.status = \"completed\"\n+                    task.result_path = result_path\n+                    queue.stats[\"tasks_completed\"] += 1\n+                    queue.stats[\"total_generation_time\"] += processing_time\n+                else:\n+                    task.status = \"failed\"\n+                    task.error_message = \"No output generated\"\n+                    queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+                    anchor.set(error=task.error_message)\n+                    queue.logger.debug(f\"Task {task.id} failed after {processing_time:.1f}s: No output generated\")\n+\n+            # Memory cleanup after each task (does NOT unload models)\n+            # This clears PyTorch's internal caches and Python garbage to prevent fragmentation\n+            queue._cleanup_memory_after_task(task.id)\n+\n+            if is_success:\n+                anchor.set(output=result_path)\n+\n+        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError, TypeError, AttributeError) as e:\n+            # Handle task failure\n+            processing_time = time.time() - start_time\n+            error_message_str = str(e)\n+\n+            with queue.queue_lock:\n+                task.status = \"failed\"\n+                task.error_message = error_message_str\n+                task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+\n+            anchor.set(error=error_message_str)\n+            queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Task {task.id} failed after {processing_time:.1f}s: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n+            queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Full traceback:\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\")\n+\n+            # DEEP DIAGNOSTIC: Log exception details for NoneType errors\n+            if isinstance(e, (TypeError, AttributeError)):\n+                queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception is {type(e).__name__} - likely attribute access on None\")\n+                queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception module: {type(e).__module__}\")\n+                queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception args: {e.args}\")\n+                if hasattr(e, '__traceback__'):\n+                    import sys\n+                    tb = e.__traceback__\n+                    queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Traceback frames:\")\n+                    frame_num = 0\n+                    while tb is not None:\n+                        frame = tb.tb_frame\n+                        queue.logger.error(f\"  Frame {frame_num}: {frame.f_code.co_filename}:{tb.tb_lineno} in {frame.f_code.co_name}\")\n+                        queue.logger.error(f\"    Local vars: {list(frame.f_locals.keys())}\")\n+                        tb = tb.tb_next\n+                        frame_num += 1\n+\n+            try:\n+                from source.core.log import flush_log_buffer\n+                flush_log_buffer()\n+            except (ImportError, AttributeError, OSError):\n+                pass  # Don't let flush errors mask the original exception\n+\n+            _check_fatal_error_and_raise(\n+                queue=queue,\n+                error_message_str=error_message_str,\n+                exception=e,\n+                task_id=task.id,\n+            )\n+        except BaseException as e:\n+            # Catch any unexpected exceptions to prevent task from silently dying\n+            processing_time = time.time() - start_time\n+            error_message_str = str(e)\n+\n+            with queue.queue_lock:\n+                task.status = \"failed\"\n+                task.error_message = error_message_str\n+                task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+\n+            anchor.set(error=error_message_str)\n+            queue.logger.critical(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Task {task.id} hit UNEXPECTED exception after {processing_time:.1f}s: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n+            queue.logger.critical(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Full traceback:\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\")\n+\n+            try:\n+                from source.core.log import flush_log_buffer\n+                flush_log_buffer()\n+            except (ImportError, AttributeError, OSError):\n+                pass  # Don't let flush errors mask the original exception\n+\n+            _check_fatal_error_and_raise(\n+                queue=queue,\n+                error_message_str=error_message_str,\n+                exception=e,\n+                task_id=task.id,\n+            )\n*** End Patch\n"
}

1. Ensure correct model is loaded (orchestrator checks WGP's ground truth)\n+                switch_start = time.time()\n+                queue._switch_model(task.model, worker_name)\n+                switch_elapsed = time.time() - switch_start\n+                # 2. Reset billing start time now that model is loaded\n+                # This ensures users aren't charged for model loading time\n+                try:\n+                    from source.core.db.task_status import reset_generation_started_at\n+                    reset_generation_started_at(task.id)\n+                    billing_reset_ok = True\n+                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_billing:\n+                    # Don't fail the task if billing reset fails - just log it\n+                    queue.logger.debug_anomaly(\"BILLING\", f\"Failed to reset generation_started_at for task {task.id}: {e_billing}\")\n+\n+                # 3. Delegate actual generation to orchestrator\n+                # The orchestrator handles the heavy lifting while we manage the queue\n+                gen_start = time.time()\n+                result_path = queue._execute_generation(task, worker_name)\n+                gen_elapsed = time.time() - gen_start\n+                queue.logger.debug(\n+                    f\"[TASK_PROCESSING] Task {task.id}: model={task.model}, switch_elapsed={switch_elapsed:.2f}s, \"\n+                    f\"billing_reset={billing_reset_ok}, generation_elapsed={gen_elapsed:.2f}s, result={result_path}\",\n+                    task_id=task.id,\n+                )\n+\n+                # Verify we're still in Wan2GP directory after generation\n+                current_dir = os.getcwd()\n+                if \"Wan2GP\" not in current_dir:\n+                    queue.logger.debug(\n+                        f\"[PATH_CHECK] After generation: Current directory changed!\\n\"\n+                        f\"  Current: {current_dir}\\n\"\n+                        f\"  Expected: Should contain 'Wan2GP'\\n\"\n+                        f\"  This may cause issues for subsequent tasks!\"\n+                    )\n+                # 3. Validate output and update task status\n+                processing_time = time.time() - start_time\n+                is_success = bool(result_path)\n+                try:\n+                    if is_success:\n+                        # If a path was returned, check existence where possible\n+                        rp = Path(result_path)\n+                        is_success = rp.exists()\n+\n+                        # Some environments (e.g. networked volumes) can briefly report a freshly-written file as missing.\n+                        # Do a short retry before failing the task.\n+                        if not is_success:\n+                            try:\n+                                # Note: os is imported at module level - don't re-import here as it causes\n+                                # \"local variable 'os' referenced before assignment\" due to Python scoping\n+                                import time as _time\n+\n+                                retry_s = 2.0\n+                                interval_s = 0.2\n+                                attempts = max(1, int(retry_s / interval_s))\n+\n+                                for _ in range(attempts):\n+                                    _time.sleep(interval_s)\n+                                    if rp.exists():\n+                                        is_success = True\n+                                        break\n+\n+                                if not is_success:\n+                                    # Tagged diagnostics to debug \"phantom output path\" failures\n+                                    # Keep output bounded to avoid log spam.\n+                                    tag = \"[TravelNoOutputGenerated]\"\n+                                    cwd = os.getcwd()\n+                                    parent = rp.parent\n+                                    queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Output path missing after generation: {result_path}\")\n+                                    queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} CWD: {cwd}\")\n+                                    try:\n+                                        queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Parent exists: {parent} -> {parent.exists()}\")\n+                                    except OSError as _e:\n+                                        queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Parent exists check failed: {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}\")\n+\n+                                    try:\n+                                        if parent.exists():\n+                                            # Show a small sample of directory contents to spot mismatched output dirs.\n+                                            entries = sorted([p.name for p in parent.iterdir()])[:50]\n+                                            queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Parent dir sample (first {len(entries)}): {entries}\")\n+                                    except OSError as _e:\n+                                        queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Parent list failed: {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}\")\n+\n+                                    # Common alternative location when running from Wan2GP/ with relative outputs\n+                                    try:\n+                                        alt_parent = Path(cwd) / \"outputs\"\n+                                        if alt_parent != parent and alt_parent.exists():\n+                                            alt_entries = sorted([p.name for p in alt_parent.iterdir()])[:50]\n+                                            queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Alt outputs dir: {alt_parent} sample (first {len(alt_entries)}): {alt_entries}\")\n+                                    except OSError as _e:\n+                                        queue.logger.error(f\"{tag} Alt outputs list failed: {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}\")\n+                            except OSError:\n+                                # Never let diagnostics break the worker loop.\n+                                pass\n+                except OSError:\n+                    # If any exception while checking, keep prior truthiness\n+                    pass\n+\n+                with queue.queue_lock:\n+                    task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                    if is_success:\n+                        task.status = \"completed\"\n+                        task.result_path = result_path\n+                        queue.stats[\"tasks_completed\"] += 1\n+                        queue.stats[\"total_generation_time\"] += processing_time\n+                    else:\n+                        task.status = \"failed\"\n+                        task.error_message = \"No output generated\"\n+                        queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+                        anchor.set(error=task.error_message)\n+                        queue.logger.debug(f\"Task {task.id} failed after {processing_time:.1f}s: No output generated\")\n+\n+                # Memory cleanup after each task (does NOT unload models)\n+                # This clears PyTorch's internal caches and Python garbage to prevent fragmentation\n+                queue._cleanup_memory_after_task(task.id)\n+\n+                if is_success:\n+                    anchor.set(output=result_path)\n+\n+            except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError, TypeError, AttributeError) as e:\n+                # Handle task failure\n+                processing_time = time.time() - start_time\n+                error_message_str = str(e)\n+\n+                with queue.queue_lock:\n+                    task.status = \"failed\"\n+                    task.error_message = error_message_str\n+                    task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                    queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+\n+                anchor.set(error=error_message_str)\n+                queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Task {task.id} failed after {processing_time:.1f}s: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n+                queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Full traceback:\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\")\n+\n+                # DEEP DIAGNOSTIC: Log exception details for NoneType errors\n+                if isinstance(e, (TypeError, AttributeError)):\n+                    queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception is {type(e).__name__} - likely attribute access on None\")\n+                    queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception module: {type(e).__module__}\")\n+                    queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Exception args: {e.args}\")\n+                    if hasattr(e, '__traceback__'):\n+                        import sys\n+                        tb = e.__traceback__\n+                        queue.logger.error(f\"[TASK_ERROR_DEEP] Traceback frames:\")\n+                        frame_num = 0\n+                        while tb is not None:\n+                            frame = tb.tb_frame\n+                            queue.logger.error(f\"  Frame {frame_num}: {frame.f_code.co_filename}:{tb.tb_lineno} in {frame.f_code.co_name}\")\n+                            queue.logger.error(f\"    Local vars: {list(frame.f_locals.keys())}\")\n+                            tb = tb.tb_next\n+                            frame_num += 1\n+\n+                try:\n+                    from source.core.log import flush_log_buffer\n+                    flush_log_buffer()\n+                except (ImportError, AttributeError, OSError):\n+                    pass  # Don't let flush errors mask the original exception\n+\n+                _check_fatal_error_and_raise(\n+                    queue=queue,\n+                    error_message_str=error_message_str,\n+                    exception=e,\n+                    task_id=task.id,\n+                )\n+            except BaseException as e:\n+                # Catch any unexpected exceptions to prevent task from silently dying\n+                processing_time = time.time() - start_time\n+                error_message_str = str(e)\n+\n+                with queue.queue_lock:\n+                    task.status = \"failed\"\n+                    task.error_message = error_message_str\n+                    task.processing_time = processing_time\n+                    queue.stats[\"tasks_failed\"] += 1\n+\n+                anchor.set(error=error_message_str)\n+                queue.logger.critical(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Task {task.id} hit UNEXPECTED exception after {processing_time:.1f}s: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n+                queue.logger.critical(f\"[TASK_ERROR] Full traceback:\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\")\n+\n+                try:\n+                    from source.core.log import flush_log_buffer\n+                    flush_log_buffer()\n+                except (ImportError, AttributeError, OSError):\n+                    pass  # Don't let flush errors mask the original exception\n+\n+                _check_fatal_error_and_raise(\n+                    queue=queue,\n+                    error_message_str=error_message_str,\n+                    exception=e,\n+                    task_id=task.id,\n+                )\n*** End Patch\n"

    "ContextVar correctness is the central invariant: if _active_anchor is not a contextvars.ContextVar (e.g. a plain threading.local or global), nested outer-anchor detection breaks and production will emit two anchor pairs per task. The outer-anchor regression test in T4 is the gate \u2014 do not land T5 without it passing.",
    "Steps 5+6+7 MUST ship together in one commit. If task_processor is wrapped before server.py is wrapped (or vice versa), or if task_claim.py still emits the raw-UUID essential while the new anchor is live, you will either double-emit or leak raw UUIDs during the intermediate state.",
    "The log interceptor at source/core/log/core.py:287-326 tags lines with the current task_id via the module-level essential/success/error/debug functions. The emitter MUST route through those functions (not print, not sys.stdout.write, not ComponentLogger bypass) or DB-side log capture silently drops lifecycle lines.",
    "Do NOT touch the model-switch essentials at source/models/wgp/model_ops.py:125, :173, :194 \u2014 those are billing-critical telemetry. Only wrap them in a DebugCard for debug-mode expansion; the essential one-liner stays.",
    "Do NOT touch the edge-function notification at source/models/wgp/model_ops.py:130 and do NOT migrate the ~50 existing debug_block callers \u2014 out of scope.",
    "Do NOT downgrade ffmpeg_fallback.py:42,100 essentials in travel \u2014 those are real anomalies, not plumbing.",
    "Sub-task lifecycle inside orchestrators (travel segments, join transitions, inpaint_frames iterations) must NOT get its own anchor. Only claimed top-level tasks claimed by server.py or dispatched through task_processor get an anchor pair.",
    "Friendly task id format is {short_task_type_slug}#{uuid_last8}. Raw UUIDs still flow to the DB and to the log interceptor via the task_id= kwarg so debugging stays possible \u2014 do not drop the task_id kwarg from logger calls.",
    "RunSummary renders as a titled DebugCard even in essential mode \u2014 intentional one-time exception to the 'cards are debug only' rule because it is the explicit deliverable. Flush from BOTH shutdown paths (server.py idle-release/KeyboardInterrupt/FatalWorkerError AND task_queue.main() signal_handler/KeyboardInterrupt/finally).",
    "Assumption that can be invalidated during implementation: tasks.py get_display_name body is trivially movable. If model_ops.py or queue_lifecycle.py import more than that function, widen the shim to re-export whatever they need rather than breaking imports.",
    "Debt watch: do not regress the worker-lifecycle cleanup try/finally in source/runtime/worker/server.py \u2014 RunSummary flush at idle-release exit must not move the try/finally boundary; flush inside the existing block, do not restructure it.",
    "Keep each new module under ~250 lines (success criterion 'should') \u2014 if DebugCard or lifecycle.py balloons, refactor rather than split.",
    "Questions left open in the plan: friendly_id format confirmed as {short_type}#{uuid_last8}; travel stitch final-video and upscale lines default to debug; DebugCard max width 120; standalone HeadlessTaskQueue.main() gets the full treatment.   "meta_commentary": "Execution order is non-negotiable for T5: server.py wrap + task_processor wrap + task_claim.py downgrade + queue generators/output.py downgrade + standalone RunSummary flush must land in ONE commit. Splitting them leaves the tree in a state where either (a) the claim line still leaks raw UUIDs alongside the new friendly anchor, or (b) standalone HeadlessTaskQueue.main() goes silent, or (c) production emits two anchor pairs per task. The contextvars.ContextVar nesting is the hinge \u2014 if it isn't a real ContextVar, every production task doubles. T4's outer-anchor regression test is the gate; run it before landing T5. Phase 3 tasks (T6-T11) are independent and can ship in any order after T5. T12 runs last and includes a throwaway repro script that MUST be deleted after it passes. Do NOT create new test files beyond the three in T4. Preserve all ffmpeg_fallback anomaly essentials, all debug_block callers (~50), and all model-switch billing telemetry at model_ops.py:125/:173/:194 \u2014 only wrap the model switch in a DebugCard for debug-mode expansion, don't touch the essentials. The log interceptor at core.py:287-326 demands that all lifecycle output flow through the module-level essential/success/error/debug functions \u2014 never print, never sys.stdout. When wrapping server.py:471-589 preserve the retry/requeue branches and the existing _display.on_task_done() finally at :586-589 \u2014 do not restructure the outer try/finally. Friendly id format is {short_task_type_slug}#{uuid_last8}; raw UUIDs still go to the DB via task_id= kwargs on logger calls.",

  ]
}
